# Aristotle

## o1 infobox: infobox philosopher

## o2 subject: Aristotle

Aristotle (; 384–322 BC) was an ancient Greek philosopher and polymath. His writings span the natural sciences, philosophy, linguistics, economics, politics, psychology, and the arts. As the founder of the Peripatetic school of philosophy in the Lyceum in Athens, he began the wider Aristotelian tradition that followed, which set the groundwork for the development of modern science.

## o66 section: Syllogism

## o67 table: One of Aristotle's types of syllogism

One of Aristotle's types of syllogismThis type of syllogism, with all three terms in 'a', is known by the traditional (medieval) mnemonic Barbara.
| In words | In termsM is the Middle (here, Men), S is the Subject (Greeks), P is the Predicate (mortal). | In equationsThe first equation can be read as 'It is not true that there exists an x such that x is a man and that x is not mortal.'
| All men are mortal. All Greeks are men. ∴ All Greeks are mortal. | M a P S a M S a P |

## o100 table: Aristotle's elements

Aristotle's elements
| Element | red/blue | green/brown | Motion | Modern state of matter
| Earth | blue | brown | Down | Solid
| Water | blue | green | Down | Liquid
| Air | red | green | Up | Gas
| Fire | red | brown | Up | Plasma
| Aether | (divine substance) | None | Circular (in heavens) | Vacuum

## o198 table: Aristotle's Scala naturae (highest to lowest)

Aristotle's Scala naturae (highest to lowest)
| Group | Examples (given by Aristotle) | Blood | Legs | Souls (Rational, Sensitive, Vegetative) | Qualities (red–blue, green–brown)
| Man | Man | with blood | 2 legs | R, S, V | red, green
| Live-bearing tetrapods | Cat, hare | with blood | 4 legs | S, V | red, green
| Cetaceans | Dolphin, whale | with blood | none | S, V | red, green
| Birds | Bee-eater, nightjar | with blood | 2 legs | S, V | red, green, except brown eggs
| Egg-laying tetrapods | Chameleon, crocodile | with blood | 4 legs | S, V | blue, green except scales, eggs
| Snakes | Water snake, Ottoman viper | with blood | none | S, V | blue, green except scales, eggs
| Egg-laying fishes | Sea bass, parrotfish | with blood | none | S, V | blue, green, including eggs
| (Among the egg-laying fishes): placental selachians | Shark, skate | with blood | none | S, V | blue, green, but placenta like tetrapods
| Crustaceans | Shrimp, crab | without | many legs | S, V | blue, green except shell
| Cephalopods | Squid, octopus | without | tentacles | S, V | blue, green
| Hard-shelled animals | Cockle, trumpet snail | without | none | S, V | blue, brown (mineral shell)
| Larva-bearing insects | Ant, cicada | without | 6 legs | S, V | blue, brown
| Spontaneously generating | Sponges, worms | without | none | S, V | blue, green or brown, from earth
| Plants | Fig | without | none | V | blue, brown
| Minerals | Iron | without | none | none | blue, brown

## o205 table-row: (Among the egg-laying fishes): placental selachians

| (Among the egg-laying fishes): placental selachians | Shark, skate | with blood | none | S, V | blue, green, but placenta like tetrapods

## o206 table-row: Crustaceans

| Crustaceans | Shrimp, crab | without | many legs | S, V | blue, green except shell

## o207 table-row: Cephalopods

| Cephalopods | Squid, octopus | without | tentacles | S, V | blue, green

## o208 table-row: Hard-shelled animals

| Hard-shelled animals | Cockle, trumpet snail | without | none | S, V | blue, brown (mineral shell)

## o209 table-row: Spontaneously generating

| Spontaneously generating | Sponges, worms | without | none | S, V | blue, green or brown, from earth

## o210 table-row: Plants

| Plants | Fig | without | none | V | blue, brown

## o211 table-row: Minerals

| Minerals | Iron | without | none | none | blue, brown

## o251 table: Virtues and their accompanying vices

Virtues and their accompanying vices
| Too little | Virtuous mean | Too much
| Humbleness | High-mindedness | Vainglory
| Lack of purpose | Right ambition | Over-ambition
| Spiritlessness | Good temper | Irascibility
| Rudeness | Civility | Obsequiousness
| Cowardice | Courage | Rashness
| Insensibility | Self-control | Intemperance
| Sarcasm | Sincerity | Boastfulness
| Boorishness | Wit | Buffoonery
| Callousness | Just resentment | Spitefulness
| Pettiness | Generosity | Vulgarity
| Meanness | Liberality | Wastefulness
